<div class="row section love-videvo">
<div class="container">
<h3><span>3 Reasons</span> Why You'll Love Videvo <div class="homepage-hearts"></div></h3>
<div class="mobile">
<button class="accordion">
<span class="homepage-s4-1"></span>
<span><strong>Free</strong> Stock Footage</span>
<i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-plus"></i>
</button>
<div class="panel">
<p class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-p3"><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-s1">Our extensive library of stock media content includes over 7600 free stock footage and <a data-saferedirecturl="https://www.google.com/url?hl=en&amp;q=https://www.videvo.net/free-motion-graphics/&amp;source=gmail&amp;ust=1537283023970000&amp;usg=AFQjCNHpOHBLJOe1_ctvYOyxEzKlv0tdIA" href="https://www.videvo.net/free-motion-graphics/" rel="noopener" target="_blank">motion graphics</a> clips, and we’re continuously adding more. We carefully curate all of our content in-house, so you can be sure you are getting the best quality content that is safe to use in your project.</span><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-apple-converted-space"> </span><u></u><u></u></p>
<p class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-p6"><u></u><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-s1">As well as the free content, we have an extended library of video and audio content, available to license when you subscribe to <a data-saferedirecturl="https://www.google.com/url?hl=en&amp;q=https://www.videvo.net/premium-plans/&amp;source=gmail&amp;ust=1537283023971000&amp;usg=AFQjCNFimNVYZUCmzfSZSQ54aACgQQxyKA" href="https://www.videvo.net/premium-plans/" rel="noopener" target="_blank">Videvo Plus</a> or <a data-saferedirecturl="https://www.google.com/url?hl=en&amp;q=https://www.videvo.net/premium-plans/&amp;source=gmail&amp;ust=1537283023971000&amp;usg=AFQjCNFimNVYZUCmzfSZSQ54aACgQQxyKA" href="https://www.videvo.net/premium-plans/" rel="noopener" target="_blank">Videvo Pro</a>.</span><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-apple-converted-space"> </span></p>
</div>
<button class="accordion middle">
<span class="homepage-s4-2"></span>
<span><strong>Simple</strong> Licensing</span>
<i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-plus"></i>
</button>
<div class="panel">
<p class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-p3"><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-s1">Simple, hassle-free licensing is what we’re all about. Most content is licensed under as Royalty-Free and our own Videvo Attribution License. When you download Royalty-Free content from Videvo, you can use that content as many times as you like and in as many projects as you like, forever. There’s also no need to credit the artist in your final production.</span><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-apple-converted-space"> </span><u></u><u></u></p>
<p class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-p6"><u></u> <u></u><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-s1">The Videvo Attribution License allows for unlimited use in the same way as Royalty-Free, but you must credit the artist in your production.</span><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-apple-converted-space"> </span><u></u><u></u><u></u> <u></u></p>
<p class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-p3"><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-s1">To learn more about our licenses, take a look at our <a data-saferedirecturl="https://www.google.com/url?hl=en&amp;q=https://www.videvo.net/blog/how-we-license-our-footage-on-videvo-net/&amp;source=gmail&amp;ust=1537283023971000&amp;usg=AFQjCNEeFi79DEef6OvaH_MeaU3HaXWdig" href="https://www.videvo.net/blog/how-we-license-our-footage-on-videvo-net/" rel="noopener" target="_blank">Licensing 101 page</a>.</span><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-apple-converted-space"> </span></p>
</div>
<button class="accordion">
<span class="homepage-s4-3"></span>
<span><strong>New Videos</strong> Added Daily</span>
<i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-plus"></i>
</button>
<div class="panel">
<p class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-p3"><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-s1">We’re constantly uploading new free stock videos and motion graphics to the Videvo archive. We work with videographers and editors from around the world to bring you a diverse and unique set of free video assets that you won’t find anywhere else.</span><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-apple-converted-space"> </span><u></u><u></u></p>
<p class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-p3"><span class="m_-2570833024943738866gmail-s1">As well as downloading content, you can also upload your own videos to the Videvo library to share with the community. In doing so, you will be helping others with their own creative projects, and you’ll feel pretty awesome for it too. What’s more, you get to decide on how your content is licensed, and whether or not you receive a credit for your work. To find out more about how you can upload, read our <a data-saferedirecturl="https://www.google.com/url?hl=en&amp;q=https://www.videvo.net/blog/a-guide-to-uploading-your-clips/&amp;source=gmail&amp;ust=1537283023971000&amp;usg=AFQjCNEJxcLho1rtSZdzx124c7ZJHE6CpQ" href="https://www.videvo.net/blog/a-guide-to-uploading-your-clips/" rel="noopener" target="_blank">Guide to Uploading</a>.</span></p>
</div>

<div class="row section more-videvo">
<h3><span>More About</span> Videvo</h3>
<div class="container">
<h5>Our Story</h5>
<div class="row">
<div class="col-xs-12 col-sm-12 col-md-12 col-lg-12">
<div class="content auto-height ps-container" id="home-page-custom-scroll">
<p class="p1"> Let’s face it, stock footage and audio assets can be expensive. So much so that not everyone is able to afford some of the high prices that some agencies demand. </p>
<p> When we set up Videvo, we wanted to create the world’s largest resource of free, safe-to-use video and audio content on the web. It is our mission to continue to strive towards this goal, providing the highest quality free content on a regular basis, available for everyone to use. </p>
<p> If you like what you see, or want to see more drop us a line at <a href="mailto:hello@videvo.net">hello@videvo.net</a> with your comments and suggestions, or use the feedback widget on the left of the page when using Videvo. All feedback, good or bad, is welcome, so don’t be shy. </p>
<div class="ps-scrollbar-x-rail" style="left: 0px; width: 633px; display: none;"><div class="ps-scrollbar-x" style="left: 0px; width: 0px;"></div></div><div class="ps-scrollbar-y-rail" style="top: 0px; height: 293px; display: inherit;"><div class="ps-scrollbar-y" style="top: 0px; height: 274px;"></div></div></div>
</div>
</div>
<div class="row">
<div class="col-lg-12">
<div class="more popular-videos">
<a href="https://www.videvo.net/about/">See More About Us</a>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="AdvancedText">
<h3><span>About</span> Videvo</h3>
<p>Videvo offers free stock videos and motion graphics for use in any project. You may use these video clips free of charge, in both personal and commercial productions. Video clips that carry the Creative Commons 3.0 license must be attributed to the original author.
<br/>
<br/>
If you have any suggestions for the site, or would like to make a request for free stock footage please contact us at: <a href="mailto:hello@videvo.net">hello@videvo.net</a> and we'll do our best to help.</p>

<div class="modal-body">
<button aria-hidden="true" class="close" data-dismiss="modal" type="button">×</button>
<h2>Lost Password</h2>
<form action="#" class="wp-user-form" method="post">
<div class="username">
<label class="lostpwd-email" for="lostpwd-email">Email: </label>
<input id="lostpwd-email" name="email" size="20" type="email"/>
</div>
<div class="login_fields">
<input class="user-submit reset-password-button" name="user-submit" tabindex="1002" type="submit" value="Reset my password"/>
</div>
</form>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<script>
    +(function($){
        $(document)
        .on('click', '#modal-lostpwd input.user-submit', function(e){
            e.preventDefault();
            var url = $('#modal-lostpwd form').attr('action');
            var data = {
                reset_password: true,
                email: $('#modal-lostpwd [name="email"]').val(),
            };
            var mail = $('#modal-lostpwd [name="email"]').val();
            $.ajax({
                url: url,
                method: 'POST',
                data: data,
            }).done(function(data){
                var appendDiv = '<div class="message success" style="color: #4F8A10; background-color: #DFF2BF;"> If a matching account was found, an email was sent to '+ mail +' to allow you to reset your password.';
                $('#modal-lostpwd .login_fields').find('.message').remove();
                $('#modal-lostpwd .login_fields').append(appendDiv);
                // if (data == 'ok') {
                //     $('#modal-lostpwd .login_fields').find('.message').remove();
                //     $('#modal-lostpwd .login_fields').append('<div class="message success" style="background: #3da257">email sent</div>');
                // } else {
                //     $('#modal-lostpwd .login_fields').find('.message').remove();
                //     $('#modal-lostpwd .login_fields').append('<div class="message error">email not found</div>');
                // }
            });
        });
    })(jQuery);
</script>

    #ModalPWD {
        width: 500px;
    }
    #ModalPWD .modal-body {
        height: 250px;
    }
    #ModalPWD .message {
        background: #FF5245;
        color: #FEFEFE;
        padding: 10px;
        margin-top: 20px;
        font-weight: 600;
    }

$(document).ready(function () {
    var intervalLikeButton = setInterval(function () {
        var active = $('.btn-shares .fb-like').children().length;
        if(active) {
            $('.btn-shares .fb-like').show();
            clearInterval(intervalLikeButton);
        }
    },2000)
})
</script>
<script>
function respMenu() {
    var x = document.getElementById("myTopnav");
    var y = document.getElementById("menu-headermenu");
    var z = document.getElementById("resp-button");
    if (x.className === "navigation inline-block") {
        x.className += " responsive";
        y.className += " responsive-menu";
        z.src = "https://www.videvo.net/wp-content/themes/ViDEVO/images/x-button.png";
    } else {
        x.className = "navigation inline-block";
        y.className = "menu";
        z.src = "https://www.videvo.net/wp-content/themes/ViDEVO/images/menu-ico.png";
    }
}
$('.menu-icon-resp').click(function(){
            $(this).toggleClass('open');
        });
</script>
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    $('#searchpcid0').click(function(){
      $('#searchpcid1').removeClass('active');
      $('#searchpcid2').removeClass('active');
      $(this).addClass('active');
    });
    $('#searchpcid1').click(function(){
      $('#searchpcid0').removeClass('active');
      $('#searchpcid2').removeClass('active');
      $(this).addClass('active');
    });
    $('#searchpcid2').click(function(){
      $('#searchpcid1').removeClass('active');
      $('#searchpcid0').removeClass('active');
      $(this).addClass('active');
    });
    $("html body .search-form.home").on('submit', function(e){
        e.preventDefault();
        var keyword = $('.search-field').val();
        var type = $('#home-page .site-description .category-selector a span.text').text();
        if (type.localeCompare('Footage') == 0)
            type = "free-stock-footage/";
        if (type.localeCompare('Motion Graphics') == 0)
            type = "free-motion-graphics/";
        if (type.localeCompare('Music') == 0)
            type = "royalty-free-music/";
        if (type.localeCompare('Sound Effects') == 0)
            type = "royalty-free-sound-effects/";
        if (type.localeCompare('All')==0 || type.indexOf('Clip Type') > -1)
            window.location = 'https://www.videvo.net/search/'+ keyword;
        else
            window.location = 'https://www.videvo.net/search/'+ keyword + '/clip_type/' + type;
    });
    $("#searchinput").keypress(function(event) {
        if ( event.which == 13 ) {
            keyword = $("#searchinput").val();
            promo = '/';
            if($("#searchinput").val().substring(0,1) == '#'){
                promo += 'promo';
                keyword = keyword.substring(1);
            }
            if($('#searchpcid1').hasClass('active')) {
                window.location = 'https://www.videvo.net/search/'+ keyword +'/cat1/';
            } else if($('#searchpcid2').hasClass('active')) {
                window.location = 'https://www.videvo.net/search/'+ keyword +'/cat2/';
            } else {
                window.location = 'https://www.videvo.net/search/'+ keyword + promo;
            }
        }
    });
});
</script>
<script defer="">
function respMenuProvisory() {
    var x = document.getElementById("myTopnavProvisory");
    var y = document.getElementById("menu-headermenuaudio-1");
    var z = document.getElementById("resp-button-provisory");
    if (x.className === "navigation inline-block") {
        x.className += " responsive2";
        y.className += " responsive-menu2";
        z.src = "https://www.videvo.net/wp-content/themes/ViDEVO/images/x-button.png";
    } else {
        x.className = "navigation inline-block";
        y.className = "menu";
        z.src = "https://www.videvo.net/wp-content/themes/ViDEVO/images/menu-ico.png";
    }
}
</script>
<script>
    function menuTrick() {
        /* This functions moves the elements of the menu to a provisory burger for resolutions 992 to 1270 */
        var menuHeader
        var totalWidthAllowed = $(".menu_bar").outerWidth(true) - $("#search-top").outerWidth() - $("#myTopnavProvisory").outerWidth() - $("#menu-headertoprightmenu2").outerWidth(true) - ($('#info-bolt:visible').length == 0 ? $('#info-bolt').outerWidth() : 0);
        var burgerVisible = 0;
        console.log("ABC");
        console.log(totalWidthAllowed);
        var curentTotalWidth = 0;
        $("#myTopnav li:not(#menu-item-3600)").each(function(index){
            curentTotalWidth += $(this).outerWidth();
            if (curentTotalWidth > totalWidthAllowed) {
                if (burgerVisible == 0) {
                    $("#myTopnavProvisory").removeClass("hidden-provisory");
                    $('.top_bar .collection').addClass('mg0');
                    burgerVisible = 1;
                    $("#myTopnav li").eq(index-1).addClass("hidden-li-menu");
                    $("#menu-headermenuaudio-1 li").eq(index-1).addClass("visible-resp-m2");
                }
                $(this).addClass("hidden-li-menu");
                $("#menu-headermenuaudio-1 li").eq(index).addClass("visible-resp-m2");
            }
            else {
                if (burgerVisible = 1) {
                    $("#myTopnavProvisory").addClass("hidden-provisory");
                    $('.top_bar .collection').removeClass('mg0');
                    burgerVisible = 0;
                    $("#myTopnav li").eq(index-1).removeClass("hidden-li-menu");
                    $("#menu-headermenuaudio-1 li").eq(index-1).removeClass("visible-resp-m2");
                }
                $(this).removeClass("hidden-li-menu");
                $("#menu-headermenuaudio-1 li").eq(index).removeClass("visible-resp-m2");
            }
        })
        console.log(curentTotalWidth);
    }
    $(document).ready(function(){
        setTimeout(function(){
            // menuTrick();
        },100);
    })
    $(window).resize(function(){
        // menuTrick();
    })
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){
        // menuTrick();
        $("#menu-headertoprightmenu2").removeClass("hide-menu-top");
    });
</script>
